We further introduce a confidence-guided geometric estimation method, incorporating confidence into RANSAC-based pose initialization and bundle adjustment to reduce the influence of unreliable regions and enhance robustness under noisy channels. Simulations show that, compared to existing SemCom and traditional seperate source and channel coding, our framework maintains high image quality while significantly improving pose estimation accuracy and 3D structural consistency.

</details>


### [71] [CLIFE: Camera-LiDAR Fusion Framework for Edge-Deployable Roadside VRU Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16154)
*Tam Bang,Hoang H. Nguyen,Lei Cheng,Lihao Guo,Siyang Cao,Hussam Abubakr,Tianya Zhang,Austin Harris,Mina Sartipi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLIFE：一种边缘原生相机-LiDAR融合框架，通过在单个嵌入式设备上集成无目标在线校准和轻量级后融合跟踪，解决遮挡、光照变化和恶劣天气条件下的VRU感知挑战，实现实时路口级应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多传感器融合系统依赖云或服务器级基础设施，无法在实际路口部署，而VRU感知在遮挡、变化光照和多样天气条件下仍然困难，特别是在严格的边缘计算和延迟约束下。

Method: CLIFE框架包含目标在线的相机-LiDAR校准和轻量级后融合跟踪，完全在单个嵌入式设备上运行，无需云卸载。它自适应地按需细化相机-LiDAR对齐，并以每帧O(N log N)的复杂度执行多传感器融合和轨迹关联。

Result: 在查塔努加12个信号控制路口的部署评估显示，融合架构在各种环境（白天、夜晚、不同天气）和交通条件下显著增强了单个传感器的感知范围和鲁棒性。后融合核心在Jetson AGX Thor上达到53.2 FPS，确保实时路口级应用的高吞吐量。

Conclusion: CLIFE通过将感知置于边缘，为下游安全应用提供了可部署的基础，同时减少了运营多路口走廊的机构的带宽和校准开销，解决了实际部署的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Reliable roadside perception of vulnerable road users (VRUs) remains challenging under occlusions, variable lighting, and diverse weather conditions, particularly under strict edge-computing and latency constraints. Existing multi-sensor fusion systems rely on cloud or server-grade infrastructure, creating a deployment gap at real-world intersections. We present CLIFE, an edge-native camera-LiDAR fusion framework that integrates targetless online calibration and lightweight late-fusion tracking entirely on a single embedded device, without cloud offloading. CLIFE adaptively refines camera-LiDAR alignment on demand and performs multi-sensor fusion and track association with O(N log N) per-frame cost. We deploy CLIFE across 12 signalized intersections in Chattanooga and conduct an in-depth evaluation at a representative intersection using synchronized camera-LiDAR data that spans diverse daytime, nighttime, and weather conditions. Our experiments demonstrate that the fusion architecture substantially enhances the perceptual range and robustness of the individual sensors under varied environmental and traffic conditions. The late-fusion core operates at 53.2 FPS on the Jetson AGX Thor, ensuring high throughput for real-time intersection-scale applications. By centering perception at the edge, CLIFE provides a deployable foundation for downstream safety applications, while reducing bandwidth and calibration overhead for agencies operating multi-intersection corridors.

</details>


### [72] [Vision-Language Assistant for Emotional Reactions to Risky Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16181)
*Harine Choi,Eun Hak Lee,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KYA系统通过实时检测危险驾驶行为并生成情感化响应，为驾驶员提供情绪智能辅助，结合视觉感知和语言模型实现安全与舒适性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶中的视觉语言系统很少考虑情感维度或真实用户体验，缺乏对驾驶员情绪需求和舒适度的关注，需要在提高安全性的同时改善驾驶体验。

Method: 采用双模块框架：视觉模块使用YOLOv8变体检测附近车辆和危险行为（如突然变道），提取相对距离、速度和预计到达时间等指标并生成结构化行为日志；语言模块基于用户定义的情感偏好（中性、幽默、分析性），利用ChatGPT-4o、Claude 3、Gemini 2.5和Copilot等大语言模型生成相应的口头响应。

Result: 通过在包含危险驾驶行为的行车记录仪视频上进行评估，以及对108名参与者的用户研究显示：所有大语言模型都获得了积极评价，但偏好因用户角色而异；YOLOv8s与ChatGPT-4o组合获得最高评分4.29/5.00。

Conclusion: KYA通过整合真实世界感知与情感自适应对话，为车载人工智能引入了情感智能新范式，为提高传统和自动驾驶车辆的安全性、信任度和情绪福祉提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: This study introduces a vision-language pipeline that detects risky driving behaviors and generates emotionally expressive responses to support driver awareness and comfort. Although vision-language models have advanced perception and reasoning in autonomous driving, existing systems rarely consider the emotional dimension or real-world user experience. Keep Yelling Assistant (KYA) detects high-risk driving maneuvers in real time, such as sudden cut-ins. It then produces emotional responses through a large language model tailored to driver preferences. The framework comprises two core modules. The vision module uses YOLOv8 variants to detect nearby vehicles and identify risky behaviors such as sudden cut-ins. Key driving metrics, including relative distance, speed, and projected reach time, are extracted and normalized to produce a structured behavior log. The language module processes this log with user-defined emotional tone settings, such as neutral, humorous, and analytical, and generates verbal reactions using state-of-the-art large language models, including ChatGPT-4o, Claude 3, Gemini 2.5, and Copilot. We evaluated the proposed system using dashcam videos containing risky driving behaviors and a user study involving 108 participants. Participants selected preferred response styles, and the large language models were evaluated based on emotional alignment. All models received favorable ratings, although preferences varied across personas. Notably, the combination of YOLOv8s and ChatGPT-4o achieved the highest score of 4.29 out of 5.00. By integrating real-world perception with emotionally adaptive dialogue, KYA introduces a new paradigm for emotionally intelligent in-vehicle artificial intelligence. It offers promising directions for improving safety, trust, and emotional well-being in both conventional and autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [73] [Searching Videos as Trees: Self-Correcting Agents for Grounded Long Video QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16189)
*Ce Zhang,Ziyang Wang,Yulu Pan,Oluwatumininu Oguntola,Pranav Wagh,Qiyu Wu,Hiromi Wakaki,Mohit Bansal,Gedas Bertasius*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoTreeSearch (VTS) 是一个用于接地长视频问答的框架，将任务建模为在自适应时间树上的迭代自校正搜索，通过四个离散操作（缩放、平移、回答）实现显式回溯和恢复机制，显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的接地长视频问答方法通常将任务建模为多轮探索，仅支持从粗到细的逐步细化，但缺乏从细到粗的显式回溯原语，导致智能体可能过早收敛且无法从早期错误中恢复。

Method: VTS框架将接地长视频问答构建为在自适应时间树上的迭代搜索过程：1) 基于视觉场景边界构建非均匀树，每个节点对应语义连贯的片段；2) 训练智能体通过四个离散操作（zoom_in、zoom_out、shift、answer）在树上导航；3) 开发轨迹合成流程生成多步路径，包括故意进入错误分支后恢复的轨迹；4) 使用监督微调加基于接地性和答案准确性的强化学习训练导航策略。

Result: 在三个接地长视频问答基准测试上显著优于先前方法：CG-Bench上mIoU提升+12.5，Haystack-Ego4D上T-F1提升+7.4。学到的策略还能迁移到通用长视频问答任务，在Video-MME、MLVU和LVBench上优于所有先前基准方法，最高提升+7.1个准确率点。消融实验证实自校正分层搜索是性能提升的核心机制。

Conclusion: VTS通过将接地长视频问答建模为在自适应时间树上的迭代自校正搜索，实现了显式的回溯和恢复能力，解决了现有方法中过早收敛和无法从错误中恢复的问题，显著提升了性能和可迁移性。

Abstract: Grounded long-video question answering (Grounded LVQA) requires answering a question about a long video while localizing the short evidence interval that supports the answer. Recent agentic methods frame this task as multi-turn exploration with a single crop_video(start, end) action, which supports coarse-to-fine narrowing but provides no primitive for fine-to-coarse backtracking. As a result, these agents typically converge prematurely and cannot recover from an early mistake. We propose VideoTreeSearch (VTS), a framework that casts grounded LVQA as iterative self-correcting search over an adaptive temporal tree. VTS constructs a non-uniform tree from visual scene boundaries so that each node corresponds to a semantically coherent segment, and trains an agent to navigate the tree through four discrete operations: zoom_in, zoom_out, shift, and answer. These operations expose backtracking and recovery as explicit, learnable primitives rather than implicit behaviors. To train this navigation, we introduce a trajectory synthesis pipeline that produces multi-step paths through the tree, including deliberate detours into incorrect branches followed by recovery. We use these trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, followed by reinforcement learning with grounding and answer-accuracy rewards. On three Grounded LVQA benchmarks (CG-Bench, Haystack-LVBench, Haystack-Ego4D), VTS outperforms the strongest prior agentic methods by +12.5 mIoU on CG-Bench and +7.4 T-F1 on Haystack-Ego4D. The learned policy also transfers to general long-video QA, surpassing all prior agentic baselines on Video-MME, MLVU, and LVBench by up to +7.1 accuracy points. Ablations confirm that self-correcting hierarchical search is the central mechanism behind these gains: removing either adaptive descent or explicit backtracking substantially degrades performance. Code is available at https://github.com/CeeZh/VTS.

</details>


### [74] [FVAttn: Adaptive Sparse Attention with Runtime Load Balancing for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16190)
*Hao Liu,Chenghuan Huang,Ye Huang,Zhiying Wen,Hao Liu,Mohan Zhang,Chen Li,Ziyang Ma,Jing Lyu,Jiangsu Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VIDAR提出了一种无需训练的稀疏注意力系统，用于改进多GPU序列并行下自适应稀疏注意力的分布式执行效率，解决了负载不均衡问题，显著提升了视频扩散变换器的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散变换器处理长时空序列时，自注意力成为高分辨率视频生成的主要瓶颈。现有的免训练稀疏注意力虽然能降低成本，但自适应Top-p路由在多GPU序列并行下会造成每头工作负载不均，导致负载异构性和rank级别的滞后问题。

Method: VIDAR采用：1）稀疏路由前端：Top-p路由、Top-k安全底线和视频感知块组织；2）运行时负载均衡：通过P2P通信迁移少量重头以缩短关键路径；3）空闲感知稀疏增强：用额外高价值块填充剩余非关键rank的空闲时间；4）重叠技术：将调度和迁移开销隐藏在现有计算后面。

Result: 在step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V上，VIDAR将平均负载不均衡从1.34降低到1.08，注意力速度相比FlashAttention提升4.41倍，DiT推理速度提升2.02-2.11倍，同时保持竞争性的视频质量。

Conclusion: VIDAR有效解决了自适应稀疏注意力在多GPU序列并行下的负载不均衡问题，通过运行时负载平衡和空闲感知稀疏增强技术，显著提升了视频扩散变换器的推理效率，为高分辨率视频生成提供了实用的系统级优化方案。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Transformers process long spatio-temporal sequences, making self-attention the main bottleneck in high-resolution video generation. Training-free sparse attention reduces this cost, but adaptive Top-$p$ routing creates uneven per-head workloads under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. The resulting workload heterogeneity turns sparse attention into a rank-level straggler problem. We present \method{}, a training-free sparse-attention system that improves the distributed execution efficiency of adaptive sparse attention under multi-GPU sequence parallelism. \method{} uses Top-$p$ routing, a Top-$k$ safety floor, and video-aware block organization as the sparse-routing frontend, then repairs the materialized mask at runtime. Runtime Load Balancing migrates a small number of heavy heads via P2P communication to shorten the current critical path. Slack-Aware Sparse Augmentation fills residual non-critical-rank slack with additional high-value blocks, while overlap hides scheduling and migration overhead behind existing computation. On step-distilled Wan2.2 I2V, \method{} reduces average load imbalance from 1.34 to 1.08 and delivers a $4.41\times$ attention speedup over FlashAttention, while achieving a $2.02$--$2.11\times$ DiT inference speedup with competitive video quality.

</details>


### [75] [MotionForesight: Re-purposing Video Models for Future 3D Scene-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16192)
*Homanga Bharadhwaj,Yash Jangir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MotionForesight是一个从单目视频学习预测人机交互中物体3D运动轨迹的系统，通过重定向视频预测模型的先验知识来实现，无需语言辅助且具有良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能够从被动观察中推断物体可能的运动方式，这种预测能力对现实世界中的行动至关重要。然而，如何从普通的人机交互单目视频中学习这种预测能力仍然是一个挑战。现有方法通常需要大量数据或辅助输入，而本文旨在探索如何高效地从视频中学习物理交互的预测能力。

Method: 1. 将交互预测建模为以物体为中心的3D运动预测，不对物体属性做任何假设；2. 利用视频预测模型已编码的丰富先验知识，将其从像素预测重定向到未来3D场景流预测；3. 基于预训练视频模型构建密集3D跟踪器，从完整视频片段生成伪地面实况轨迹；4. 使用学习到的掩码潜在表示替换未来RGB和几何信息，训练轻量级适配器将回顾性跟踪表示转换为前向预测器，同时冻结大型视频和跟踪组件。

Result: MotionForesight仅使用4万个人类视频且无语言等辅助输入，就能在分布外物体、环境、视角和交互中表现出良好的泛化能力。它在性能上明显优于使用超过百万训练视频的大规模模型，展示了将视频先验高效重用于显式几何预测的可行性。

Conclusion: 视频预测模型确实编码了丰富的人机交互运动先验知识，这些先验可以被高效地重定向到显式几何预测任务中，为具身智能提供了有效的运动预测解决方案，展示了从小规模数据中学习复杂物理交互预测的潜力。

Abstract: Humans can infer how objects are likely to move from passive observation: a cup may be lifted, a drawer may slide, and a lid may rotate shut. Such predictions expose the physical consequences of interaction needed to act in the real world. We study how to learn this anticipation from ordinary monocular videos of human-object interaction. Given a short observed video context, MotionForesight predicts future 3D trajectories for points on the manipulated object. This casts interaction prediction as object-centered 3D motion forecasting without any assumptions on the object properties. Our key insight is that video prediction models already encode rich priors about how objects move during human interactions. We redirect these priors from pixel prediction toward future 3D scene flow. We start from a dense 3D tracker built on a pretrained video model, generate pseudo-ground-truth tracks from complete clips, and train the forecaster using only the observed frames. We replace future RGB and geometry with learned mask latents and train a lightweight adapter to turn the retrospective tracking representation into a forward predictor, while freezing the large video and tracking components. Using just 40k human videos and no auxiliary inputs such as language, MotionForesight generalizes across diverse out-of-distribution objects, environments, viewpoints, and interactions. It also outperforms substantially larger models that use over a million training videos. These results show that we can efficiently re-purpose video priors into explicit geometric forecasts for embodied intelligence. https://motionforesight.github.io/

</details>


### [76] [Knowing the Self, Understanding the World: A Dual-Cognition Benchmark for UAV Spatio-temporal Reasoning with MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16193)
*Like Liu,Zhengzheng Xu,Haitao He,Hongzhe Li,Shuchang Zhang,Dian Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UAV-DualCog是一个针对无人机场景的双认知能力评估基准，要求模型同时推理无人机自身状态和外部环境状态，并在多视角时空上下文中进行空间或时间定位，超越传统的离散答案预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无人机导向基准主要关注场景理解、事件识别或导航完成等单一任务，缺乏对无人机智能体所需双认知能力（同时推理自身状态和环境状态）的联合评估。多模态大语言模型在无人机场景中的能力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 1. 提出UAV-DualCog基准，从双认知视角构建，包含图像和视频任务来联合评估自状态和环境状态推理；2. 开发自动化流水线从场景级语义点云构建数据，产生可扩展的基准，包含多样场景、数百个地标和数千个QA样本；3. 构建UAV-DualCog-Train用于训练，并通过轻量级优化探针验证其作为结构化监督数据的价值。

Result: 1. 现有MLLMs在无人机双认知任务中表现远不可靠；2. 自状态推理、视角变换、精确空间定位和时间间隔定位是持续存在的瓶颈；3. 人类基准验证表明基准对人类可理解但对现有模型具有挑战性；4. UAV-DualCog-Train提供有用的结构化监督，表明其不仅可作为评估基准，也可作为推进MLLM基无人机智能体的数据资源。

Conclusion: UAV-DualCog填补了无人机多模态推理评估的空白，揭示了现有MLLMs在无人机双认知任务中的局限性，并为未来研究提供了有价值的评估基准和训练数据资源，有助于推进基于MLLM的无人机智能体发展。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have achieved strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, yet their capabilities in UAV scenarios remain insufficiently explored. Recent UAV-oriented benchmarks have begun to evaluate MLLMs in aerial scenarios, but they typically focus on scene understanding, event recognition, or navigation completion, rather than jointly assessing the dual-cognition capability required for UAV agents: reasoning about both the UAV's own state and the external environment in multiview spatio-temporal contexts. To address this gap, we present UAV-DualCog, a benchmark for aerial multiview spatio-temporal reasoning built on this dual-cognition perspective. UAV-DualCog includes both image and video tasks to jointly evaluate self-state and environment-state reasoning, while requiring spatial or temporal grounding beyond discrete answer prediction. We also develop an automated pipeline that constructs data from scene-level semantic point clouds, yielding a scalable benchmark with diverse scenes, hundreds of landmarks, and thousands of QA samples. Extensive evaluations show that current MLLMs remain far from reliable in UAV dual cognition. Self-state reasoning, viewpoint transformation, precise spatial grounding, and temporal interval localization are persistent bottlenecks, and additional validation with thinking/frontier models and a human baseline confirms that the benchmark is understandable to humans but challenging for existing models. We further construct UAV-DualCog-Train from disjoint scenes and show through a lightweight optimization probe that it provides useful structured supervision, suggesting its value not only as an evaluation benchmark but also as a data resource for advancing MLLM-based UAV agents. Project website and supplementary materials: https://uav-dualcog.lozumi.com

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Large Language Models as Unified Multimodal Learners for Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15380)
*Ajay Madhavan Ravichandran,Bilgin Osmandoja,Klemens Budde,Klaus Netter,Tobias Strapatsas,Aljoscha Burchardt,Sebastian Möller,Roland Roller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 将所有临床数据（文本和结构化数据）统一转化为单一自然语言序列，无需任务特定的融合架构，通过微调预训练语言模型进行多模态临床预测，在三个不同临床任务中性能匹配或优于专用基线模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录包含自由文本临床叙述和结构化测量数据，但现有临床预测系统需要为每个任务和临床设置重新设计专门的多模态融合架构，过程复杂且耗时。需要更简单通用的方法，减少系统复杂性同时保持性能。

Method: 提出统一文本序列化方法：将所有患者数据（无论模态）转换为单一自然语言序列，直接微调预训练语言模型（包括编码器模型ModernBERT和解码器模型Llama 3.1、Gemma、DeepSeek-R1-Qwen、Qwen3），无需针对任务修改融合架构。

Result: 在三个不同临床预测任务中评估：MIMIC-III的院内死亡率预测、德国移植中心数据的移植失败预测、急救车记录的紧急分诊分类。统一文本序列化方法匹配或优于多模态基线，在移植失败预测中超越了临床实践中使用的梯度提升系统。

Conclusion: 单一的序列化范式无需定制的融合架构即可实现多模态临床预测，显著降低系统复杂性，同时匹配或优于专门设计的系统。表明预训练语言模型通过统一文本表示能够有效处理多模态临床数据。

Abstract: Electronic health records combine free-text clinical narratives with structured measurements such as vital signs, laboratory values, and comorbidities. Yet most clinical prediction systems still rely on task-specific fusion architectures, pairing dedicated encoders for each modality with learned combination mechanisms that must be re-engineered for every new task and clinical setting. We propose a simpler alternative: convert all patient data, regardless of modality, into a single natural language sequence and fine-tune a pretrained language model end-to-end, with no architectural modification for fusion. We evaluate this approach across three clinically distinct prediction tasks: in-hospital mortality on MIMIC-III, graft failure prediction using longitudinal data from a German transplant center, and emergency triage classification from ambulance records - comparing encoder-based (ModernBERT) and decoder-based (Llama 3.1, Gemma, DeepSeek-R1-Qwen, Qwen3) fine-tuning against established multimodal baselines and, for graft failure, a gradient boosting model currently used in clinical practice for post-transplant patient management. Across all three tasks, unified textual serialization matches or exceeds task-specific multimodal baselines, and outperforms the clinically deployed gradient boosting system on graft failure prediction. These results indicate that a single serialization-based paradigm, without bespoke fusion architectures, is sufficient for multimodal clinical prediction - substantially reducing system complexity while matching or exceeding specialized designs.

</details>


### [78] [Verbalizable Representations Form a Global Workspace in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15495)
*Wes Gurnee,Nicholas Sofroniew,Adam Pearce,Mateusz Piotrowski,Isaac Kauvar,Runjin Chen,Anna Soligo,Paul Bogdan,Euan Ong,Rowan Wang,Ben Thompson,David Abrahams,Subhash Kantamneni,Emmanuel Ameisen,Joshua Batson,Jack Lindsey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型中存在类似于人类意识访问的功能性区分 - 通过Jacobian透镜识别出的J空间表征具有全局工作空间的特征，能够揭示模型未说出的思维过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类大脑处理的信息中只有一小部分能够被意识访问（可用于语言报告、有意控制和灵活推理），研究旨在探索类似的功能区分是否存在于大型语言模型中。

Method: 开发了一种新的可解释性技术"Jacobian透镜"，用于识别模型在其处理过程中随时准备言语化的表征，这些表征集合称为J空间。通过分析J空间的功能和结构特性，并应用于对齐审计。

Result: J空间展现出全局工作空间的特性：内容可报告、可有意召唤和保持、用于沉默推理的中间步骤、作为参数传递给任意下游计算。结构上具有中间层特征、同时容纳数十个概念、通过模型权重更广泛广播。在审计中揭示了战略思考、评估意识和训练出的未对齐倾向。后训练将助手视角安装到工作空间，反事实反思训练改进行为。

Conclusion: 语言模型维护着一小部分特权表征，具有意识访问的功能特征，解码这些表征能够揭示正在进行的认知过程，这为理解模型内部思维提供了实用窗口。

Abstract: Out of everything the human brain processes, only a small fraction is consciously accessible, in the sense of being available for verbal report, deliberate control, and flexible reasoning. In this paper, we present evidence that an analogous functional distinction has emerged in large language models. Using a new interpretability technique, the Jacobian lens, we identify the representations a model is poised to verbalize at any point in its processing. These representations, which we collectively call the J-space, exhibit the functional properties characteristic of a global workspace: their contents can be reported, deliberately summoned and held, used to carry the intermediate steps of silent reasoning, and passed as arguments to arbitrary downstream computations, while automatic processing such as text parsing and routine inference proceeds without them. The J-space also has structural signatures that global workspace theory associates with conscious access: it carries coherent content only in an intermediate band of layers, holds on the order of tens of concepts at a time, and is broadcast by the model's weights more widely than other representations. These properties make it a practical window into a model's unspoken thinking. In alignment audits, it reveals strategic deliberation, evaluation awareness, and trained-in misaligned dispositions that never appear in the model's outputs. We find that post-training installs the Assistant's point of view in the workspace, and we introduce counterfactual reflection training, which improves behavior by training only what a model would say if interrupted and asked to reflect. These results indicate that language models maintain a small, privileged set of representations bearing some of the functional hallmarks of conscious access, and that decoding these representations sheds light on ongoing cognitive processes.

</details>


### [79] [VarRate: Training-Free Variable-Rate KV Cache Compression for Long-Context LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15498)
*Shahrzad Esmat,Dhawal Shah,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VarRate是一种无需训练、基于可变低秩分配的KV缓存压缩方法，通过根据查询相关性为每个令牌分配不同低秩预算来优化内存使用，在保持每个令牌非零秩的同时显著提升长上下文LLM推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前长上下文LLM推理中的KV缓存是主要内存瓶颈。现有的无需训练压缩方法存在结构性问题：1）令牌选择方法（如SnapKV、Ada-KV）通过观察窗口评分后丢弃低分令牌，但当查询无关重用时重要性信号退化会导致精度下降11-15个点；2）均匀低秩编码虽然保留每个令牌但均等地分配秩，浪费预算。两种方法失败的共同原因是需要分配秩而非丢弃令牌。

Method: VarRate是一种无需训练的KV编解码器，根据每个令牌的查询相关性为其分配可变的低秩预算，保持每个令牌都有非零秩。该方法避免了完全丢弃任何令牌，同时实现秩的智能分配。相比之下，类似的自适应秩编解码器需要通过训练才能达到这种分配，而VarRate无需训练。它在预填充阶段的开销约为现有方法的1/8。

Result: 在LongBench（16个任务）上20%内存预算匹配测试中，VarRate在Llama-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B模型上仅比未压缩模型下降0.8个点。在两个模型上的平均表现是最强的匹配内存压缩器。相比均匀秩消融实验，它在两个模型上都显著胜出。与专为查询无关重用设计的KVzip方法相比，在四分之三的场景中精度相当，总体差距在1个点以内，而预填充开销约为KVzip的1/8。

Conclusion: VarRate通过可变低秩分配解决了现有KV缓存压缩方法的结构性限制，在不丢弃任何令牌的前提下实现了高效的内存使用。相比查询感知的选择方法，精度下降仅3.5-5.5个点而非11-15个点。该方法无需训练即可达到自适应秩分配，在实际应用中具有显著优势，特别是预填充开销远低于专用方法。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the main memory bottleneck in long-context large language model (LLM) inference. Two leading training-free families are both structurally limited: token-selection methods (SnapKV, Ada-KV) score importance from an observation window and evict low-scoring tokens, but eviction is irreversible -- so when the importance signal degrades under query-agnostic reuse, accuracy collapses by 11-15 points; uniform low-rank coding keeps every token but spends equal rank everywhere, wasting budget. We observe that both failures share one cure: rank should be allocated, not evicted. We present VarRate, a training-free KV codec that assigns each token a variable low-rank budget by its query salience, keeping every token at a nonzero rank. Comparable adaptive-rank codecs reach this allocation only through training; VarRate requires none. Because no token is dropped, it degrades by only 3.5-5.5 points where query-aware selection collapses. At a matched 20% budget on LongBench (16 tasks), VarRate stays within 0.8 points of the uncompressed model on both Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B. Averaged over the two, it is the strongest matched-memory compressor. It significantly beats its uniform-rank ablation on both models. Against KVzip, a method purpose-built for query-agnostic reuse, it is accuracy-equivalent in three of four settings and within a point overall, at about one-eighth the prefill overhead.

</details>


### [80] [EpiNarrate: Agentic Generation of Grounded Narratives from Epidemiological Scenario Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15544)
*Rituparna Datta,Srini Venkatramanan,Bryan L. Lewis,Yiqi Su,Harry Hochheiser,Lucie Contamin,Parantapa Bhattacharya,Naren Ramakrishnan,Anil Vullikanti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EpiNarrate是一个用于公共卫生报告生成的智能体框架，通过分离结构化数值推理与自然语言生成，解决大型语言模型在总结复杂流行病学数据时的不一致性和遗漏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成清晰可访问的公共卫生叙述对于向政策制定者和公众传达复杂的流行病学预测至关重要。现有方法中，直接使用大型语言模型总结和情境化此类数据往往导致不一致、遗漏和脆弱行为。

Method: 提出EpiNarrate框架：1)提取情景轴并组织成偏序模式，系统遍历底层多维空间；2)构建增强数据集，通过比较语法推导有效的定量陈述；3)基于最大熵原理引入有趣性驱动的选择机制，平衡覆盖范围和非冗余性。

Result: 在COVID-19情景建模中心上的实验表明，该模型生成的叙述具有改进的事实基础，对显著流行病学模式的覆盖范围更广，同时保持了专家撰写报告的风格。

Conclusion: EpiNarrate通过分离数值推理和语言生成，提供了一种系统方法来生成定量基础和上下文丰富的公共卫生叙述，显著改善了从复杂流行病学数据到可访问报告的转换质量。

Abstract: Generation of clear and accessible public health narratives is critical for communicating complex epidemiological projections to policymakers and the general public at large. Such narratives require more than simply reporting numbers: projections must be contextualized and quantitatively grounded across multiple dimensions. Further, projections are often derived from large ensemble datasets which combine intervention assumptions, geographic and demographic strata, outcomes, time horizons, and uncertainty quantiles. However, directly using large language models (LLMs) to summarize and contextualize such data often leads to inconsistencies, omissions, and fragile behavior. We introduce an agentic framework (EpiNarrate) for public health report generation that separates structured numerical reasoning from natural-language generation. The framework first extracts scenario axes and organizes them into a partial-order schema, enabling systematic traversal of the underlying multidimensional space. It then constructs an augmented dataset and derives valid quantitative statements through a comparison grammar that enforces semantic and arithmetic consistency. To balance coverage and non-redundancy, we introduce an interestingness-driven selection mechanism based on maximum-entropy principles. Experiments on the COVID-19 Scenario Modeling Hub demonstrate that our model produces narratives with improved factual grounding and broader coverage of salient epidemiological patterns, while preserving the style of expert-written reports.

</details>


### [81] [SkillCorpus: Consolidating and Evaluating the Open Skill Ecosystem for Real-World LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15557)
*Yanze Wang,Pengfei Yao,Tianyi Sun,Chuanrui Hu,Yan Xiao,Yunyun Han,Jun Sun,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillCorpus是一个框架，用于聚合、策划、匹配和评估开源SKILL.md生态系统，通过多阶段流程从82.1万个技能中筛选出9.6万个高质量技能，并通过优化的检索-选择堆栈为LLM代理提供任务相关技能，在三个基准测试中带来一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前公共仓库中大量存在的SKILL.md文件（为LLM代理打包可重用程序性知识的文件）存在碎片化、冗余和质量参差不齐的问题，其实际价值不明确。核心问题是如何将这些开源技能整合成单一可用的语料库，以及其在真实世界代理任务中的效益边界。

Method: SkillCorpus框架包含四个主要组件：1) 从公共仓库爬取约82.1万个技能；2) 通过多阶段管道筛选，最终保留96,401个技能；3) 使用16类分类法和三个质量维度（实用性、鲁棒性、安全性）组织技能；4) 配以经过微调的检索-选择堆栈，用于匹配任务相关技能。

Result: 在三个基准测试（SkillsBench、GDPVal、QwenClawBench）、两个测试框架和两个开放骨干模型上进行评估，并进行前沿鲁棒性检查。整合SkillCorpus在所有三个基准测试中带来一致的性能提升，其中在SkillsBench上提升最大（+7.5个百分点）。操作分析将增益归因于覆盖边界和测试框架边界。

Conclusion: SkillCorpus是首个完整描述策划的、通过检索服务的社区语料库何时改进真实代理任务以及何时无法改进的端到端系统。该研究提供了对开源技能生态系统整合和实用化的系统性解决方案，数据集、模型和代码将在论文接受后发布。

Abstract: Agent skills, SKILL.md files that package reusable procedural knowledge for an LLM agent, are a popular mechanism for extending agent capabilities. Public repositories now host them in large and growing numbers, yet these artifacts are fragmented, redundant, and uneven in quality, and their value in practice is unclear. A core question remains open, namely how to consolidate this open-source SKILL.md ecosystem into a single usable corpus, and what bounds its benefit on real-world agent tasks. We present SkillCorpus, a framework that aggregates, curates, matches, and evaluates the open skill ecosystem at scale. It filters ~821,000 crawled skills through a multi-stage pipeline into 96,401 skills organised by a 16-class taxonomy and three quality facets (utility, robustness, safety), and pairs them with a fine-tuned retrieval-and-selection stack that matches task-relevant skills. We evaluate end-to-end across three benchmarks (SkillsBench, GDPVal, QwenClawBench), two harnesses, and two open backbones with a frontier robustness check. Integrating SkillCorpus yields consistent gains across all three benchmarks, largest on SkillsBench (+7.5 pp). An operational analysis traces the gains to a coverage boundary and a harness boundary. SkillCorpus is, to our knowledge, the first end-to-end account of when a curated, retrieval-served community corpus improves real agent tasks, and where it does not. The dataset, models, and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [82] [Process Reward Informed Tree Rollout for Effective Multi-Turn RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15610)
*Xintong Li,Sha Li,Yuwei Zhang,Changlong Yu,Rongmei Lin,Hongye Jin,Shuyi Guan,Xin Liu,Linwei Li,Qingyu Yin,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PATR（Process-Scorer Guided Adaptive Tree Rollout）是一种用于多轮智能体强化学习的新型自适应树展开框架，通过过程反馈评分选择性地从有希望的状态分支，重用共享前缀，并保守地停止退化路径，从而提高采样效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL方法（如GRPO/RLOO）在长视野智能体任务中采用均匀的轨迹采样策略，浪费预算在无信息的死胡同尝试上，而有希望的中间状态没有得到充分探索。多轮智能体轨迹的树结构视角自然支持将轨迹组组织为树，每个轮次作为探索的决策点。

Method: PATR使用任务适当的过程反馈来评分部分轨迹，选择性地从有希望的状态分支，重用共享前缀，并保守地停止退化路径以减少浪费的采样。生成的展开组保持与标准策略优化的兼容性，同时在相同训练预算下提供更有效的探索。

Result: 在FrozenLake和挑战性的SWE-Bench上进行评估，PATR在SWE-Bench上性能提升高达+5.0点，在FrozenLake上提升+9.3点。SWE-Bench此前基本未被树展开智能体RL方法探索过。

Conclusion: 过程引导的树展开是用于可扩展多轮RL的有效策略。PATR通过质量感知的展开框架，在保持与标准策略优化兼容的同时，显著提高了采样效率和性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key approach for training LLM agents, yet popular methods such as GRPO/RLOO rely on multiple independently sampled complete trajectories for advantage estimation. In long-horizon agentic tasks, such a uniform rollout strategy can waste budget on uninformative dead-end attempts, while promising intermediate states do not receive sufficient exploration. The multi-turn structure of agentic trajectories, with interleaved actions and observations, naturally supports organizing a trajectory group as a tree, where each turn serves as a decision point for exploration. This perspective reframes effective exploration as the problem of deciding where to branch. We propose Process-Scorer Guided Adaptive Tree Rollout (PATR), a quality-aware rollout framework for multi-turn agent RL. PATR uses task-appropriate process feedback to score partial trajectories, selectively branches from promising states, reuses shared prefixes, and conservatively stops degenerate paths to reduce wasted sampling. The resulting rollout groups remain compatible with standard policy optimization while providing more efficient exploration under the same training budget. We evaluate PATR on FrozenLake and the challenging SWE-Bench, which is largely unexplored by prior tree-rollout agent RL methods. Experiments show that PATR improves performance by up to +5.0 points on SWE-Bench and +9.3 points on FrozenLake, highlighting process-guided tree rollouts as an effective strategy for scalable multi-turn RL.

</details>


### [83] [On the Structure of Address in Multi-Party Dialogue: From Discrete Labels to Continuous Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15648)
*Taiga Mori,Koji Inoue,Divesh Lala,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视多参与者对话中的受话人识别问题，将其从传统的离散多类分类任务重新定义为连续现象，通过潜变量模型构建连续地址级别，并分析其与话轮转换、注视和反馈信号的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究将受话人检测视为多类分类任务，选择单个标签代表个人或群体参与者，这种方法假设地址是离散的，主要用于预测话轮转换。本文质疑这一假设，认为地址可能具有连续特性。

Method: 使用多人对话语料库，构建两种表示：1）基于多数投票的二值化地址标签，2）使用潜变量模型从标注者判断推断的连续地址级别。然后分析这些表示与话轮转换、注视行为和反馈信号的关联。

Result: 结果表明，除了话轮转换外，注视和反馈信号都与地址相关。使用连续地址级别的模型比使用离散标签的模型具有更好的预测拟合度，表明地址可能表现出分级结构。

Conclusion: 地址在多参与者对话中可能具有连续性质，连续表示比离散表示更能捕捉对话动态。这一发现为受话人检测研究的未来发展提供了新的方向，强调了考虑地址分级结构的重要性。

Abstract: In multi-party dialogues between a dialogue system and multiple users, identifying to whom an utterance is addressed is a key challenge. Prior work has typically treated addressee detection as a multi-class classification task, selecting a single label representing an individual participant or the group. This formulation assumes that address is inherently discrete and has primarily been used for predicting turn-taking. In this paper, we revisit this assumption by analyzing address as a continuous phenomenon. Using a multi-party human dialogue corpus annotated by multiple annotators, we construct both binary address labels derived from majority-vote addressee labels and continuous address levels inferred from annotator judgments using a latent-variable model. We then examine how these representations relate to turn-taking as well as listener behaviors, including gaze and backchannels. Our results show that, in addition to turn-taking, both gaze and backchannels are associated with address. Furthermore, models using continuous address levels achieve better predictive fit than those using discrete labels, suggesting that address may exhibit graded structure. Finally, we discuss the future directions of addressee detection research based on the findings of this study.

</details>


### [84] [Adaptive Multi-Step Lookahead Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15655)
*Yingqian Cui,Wei Deng,Lantao Mei,Hang Li,Charu C. Aggarwal,Hui Liu,Yue Xing*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaLook是一个用于掩码扩散语言模型解码的自适应前瞻框架，通过动态决定是否继续展开和分支扩展来优化准确性与解码步数的权衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于前瞻的解码方法主要依赖浅层一步前瞻，虽然优化了即时信息增益，但对更长的解码轨迹可能是次优的。而简单扩展更深前瞻也无效，因为固定深度展开引入了额外计算且无法适应异构的中间解码状态

Method: AdaLook动态决定是否继续展开基于候选分数方差，并在中间展开状态需要额外探索时启用分支扩展。该设计避免了不必要的深度展开，同时允许解码器从信息丰富的中间状态重新触发前瞻

Result: 在各种基准测试和模型上的实验表明，AdaLook比现有的一步前瞻解码方法实现了更好的准确性-解码步数权衡

Conclusion: AdaLook框架有效解决了掩码扩散语言模型解码中的适应性前瞻问题，通过动态决策机制和分支扩展策略提升了解码效率与质量的平衡

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (DLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively refining masked tokens, offering a promising alternative to autoregressive decoding. Recent lookahead-based decoding methods improve the accuracy--efficiency trade-off by exploring future decoding states before committing token updates. However, existing approaches mainly rely on shallow one-step lookahead, which optimizes immediate information gain but can be suboptimal for longer-horizon decoding trajectories. Meanwhile, we find that a naive extension for deeper lookahead is also ineffective, as fixed-depth rollout introduces additional computation and cannot adapt to heterogeneous intermediate decoding states. Thus, in this work, we propose AdaLook, an adaptive lookahead framework for DLM decoding. AdaLook dynamically determines whether to continue rollout based on candidate-score variance and further enables branch expansion when intermediate rollout states require additional exploration. This design avoids unnecessary deep rollout while allowing the decoder to re-trigger lookahead from informative intermediate states. Experiments on various benchmarks and models demonstrate that AdaLook achieves a better accuracy--decoding steps trade-off than existing one-step lookahead decoding methods.

</details>


### [85] [Better Starts, Better Ends: Bootstrapped Iterative Self-Reasoning Distillation for Compressed Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15736)
*Leichao Dong,Dongxu Zhang,Yiding Sun,Qirui Wang,Yuhan Wang,Lin Chen,Jihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BIRD是一种两阶段自推理蒸馏方法，通过先改善前缀分布再进行策略训练，解决了现有自蒸馏方法的初始化瓶颈，在保持高准确率的同时显著减少了推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有策略自蒸馏方法存在初始化瓶颈问题：从冗长的基础模型开始训练时，KL损失应用于通常嘈杂、冗余或已偏离正确轨道的上下文区域，简洁教师只能提供局部修正，而学生继续探索高效推理器应避免的轨迹，这限制了蒸馏效果。

Method: BIRD采用两阶段自推理蒸馏方法：第一阶段从基础模型在简洁指令下采样简洁解决方案，仅保留答案正确的轨迹，并进行轻量级提示切换SFT步骤；第二阶段从预热模型出发，在更干净、信息更丰富的前缀上应用带有简洁自教师的策略反向KL蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen3系列模型上，BIRD在MATH-500和AIME基准测试中比提示法和冷启动策略蒸馏实现了更强的准确率-效率权衡。在Qwen3-8B上，将MATH-500准确率从86.2%提升至92.0%，同时将平均响应长度从3,099个标记减少到1,115个标记。

Conclusion: BIRD方法通过两阶段蒸馏有效解决了自蒸馏中的初始化瓶颈问题，将指令诱导的简洁性转化为默认推理行为，从而在保持准确性的同时显著提升了推理效率。研究结果表明，前缀分布的质量是高效推理蒸馏的核心因素，为未来大语言模型的高效推理优化提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Large reasoning models often solve problems through long chain-of-thought (CoT) traces, yet much of this computation is spent on redundant derivations, repeated self-verification, and detours that do not improve the final answer. Existing on-policy self-distillation methods reduce this cost by matching a student model to a concise copy of itself on prefixes sampled from the student's own rollouts. We show that this objective has an initialization bottleneck. Since supervision is applied only to visited prefixes, training from a verbose base model places the KL loss on contexts that are often noisy, redundant, or already off track. In such regions, a concise teacher can provide only local corrections, while the student continues to explore trajectories that an efficient reasoner should avoid. In this paper, we propose BIRD(Bootstrapped Iterative Self-Reasoning Distillation), a two-stage self-reasoning distillation method that improves the rollout distribution before on-policy training. BIRD first samples concise solutions from the base model under a brevity instruction, keeps only answer-correct traces, and performs a lightweight prompt-switch SFT step. The traces are generated with the brevity instruction but learned under the original task prompt, turning instruction-induced conciseness into a default reasoning behavior. Starting from this warm model, BIRD then applies on-policy reverse-KL distillation with a concise self-teacher, now on cleaner and more informative prefixes. Across Qwen3 series models, BIRD achieves a stronger accuracy-efficiency trade-off than prompting and cold-start on-policy distillation on MATH-500 and AIME benchmarks. On Qwen3-8B, it improves MATH-500 accuracy from 86.2% to 92.0% while reducing the average response length from 3,099 to 1,115 tokens. These results highlight prefix support as a central factor in efficient reasoning distillation.

</details>


### [86] [Before the Action: Benchmarking LLMs on Prospective Hypothesis Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15766)
*Tianyun Zhong,Wangyi Jiang,Wei Wang,Xuanang Chen,Yaojie Lu,Shiwei Ye,Yuzhen Shi,Boyu Yang,Jinghang Wang,Han Li,Weiqi Zhai,Bing Zhao,Hu Wei,Haiyang Yu,Yongbin Li,Hongyu Lin,Le Sun,Xianpei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了前瞻性假设发现（PHD）任务，用于评估大语言模型在证据不完整、结论未明确的情况下自主构建可检验假设空间的能力。作者开发了HypoArena评估框架，包含988个案例的数据集和开放式假设评估方法。实验揭示了LLM在该任务上的能力分层和结构化分析技能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在回答预设问题方面表现出色，但其在开放式的、结论尚未明确的发现阶段（即前瞻性假设发现）的能力尚未得到充分评估。现有的评估主要关注模型在已有明确结论或假设的情况下的表现，而忽视了模型在探索性研究中从零碎、不完整证据中自主构建假设空间的能力。

Method: 作者提出了前瞻性假设发现（PHD）任务，要求模型从不完整证据（包括异常观测和零碎记录）中自主构建有依据、可区分且可检验的假设空间。为了评估这一能力，开发了HypoArena评估框架，包含HypoData（988个案例的基准数据集，涵盖6个科学和分析领域）和HypoEval（开放式假设集的评估框架）。通过"回顾性上下文回归"方法大规模构建HypoData，采用Forge-Audit管道从专家完成的文档中重建结论前的上下文（移除明确结论、目标假设和回顾性因果归因，同时保留事实基础）。由于PHD允许多种有效输出，HypoEval结合了双向配对判断与Bradley-Terry-Davidson聚合进行排名，以及六维标准评分进行诊断分析。

Result: 对15个前沿大语言模型的实验揭示了清晰的能力分层，以及结构化分析技能对模型性能的依赖效应：部分低性能模型在HypoArena上有所提升，而其他系统（包括一个顶级模型）表现退步。与绝对标准评分相比，竞技场式评估能够分辨模型间更细微的差异，且聚合排名与人类专家和独立评委的判断具有较强的一致性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，前瞻性假设发现应被视为评估大语言模型在最终结论被隐瞒时如何制定调查方向的独特目标。这项工作为评估LLM在探索性研究中的能力提供了新的框架和基准，并公开了代码和数据集。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at answering pre-specified questions, yet their ability to navigate the open-ended, pre-conclusion stage of discovery remains largely unmeasured. We introduce Prospective Hypothesis Discovery (PHD), which asks models to autonomously construct grounded, discriminative, and testable hypothesis spaces from inconclusive evidence, including anomalous observations and fragmented records, to guide subsequent investigation. To evaluate this capability, we introduce HypoArena, comprising HypoData, a benchmark of 988 cases across six scientific and analytical domains, and HypoEval, an evaluation framework for open-ended hypothesis sets. To construct HypoData at scale, we propose Retrospective Context Regression, a Forge--Audit pipeline that reconstructs pre-conclusion contexts from completed expert documents by removing explicit conclusions, target hypotheses, and retrospective causal attributions while preserving the factual substrate. Because PHD admits multiple valid outputs, HypoEval combines bidirectional pairwise judgments with Bradley--Terry--Davidson aggregation for ranking and six-dimensional rubric scoring for diagnosis. Experiments on 15 frontier LLMs reveal clear capability stratification and model-dependent effects of structured analytical skills, with gains for several lower-performing models on HypoArena but regressions for other systems, including a top-performing model. Compared with absolute rubric scoring, arena evaluation resolves finer-grained differences among models, with aggregated rankings showing strong agreement with human experts and an independent judge. Together, these results support treating PHD as a distinct target for evaluating how LLMs formulate investigative directions when final conclusions are withheld. Our code and data are publicly available at github.com/SKYLENAGE-AI/HypoArena and github.com/SKYLENAGE-AI/HypoArena.

</details>


### [87] [Cost-efficient generative AI summarization for scalable automated essay scoring in educational assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15829)
*Haowei Hua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于生成式AI的摘要框架来解决自动作文评分中长文本处理问题，通过GPT-5变体生成摘要并结合手工特征，在ASAP 2.0数据集上评估评分性能、摘要质量和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化作文评分面临变压器模型输入长度限制的问题，处理长作文时会导致信息丢失。当前方法难以在有限输入长度内充分表征长文本内容，影响评分可靠性。

Method: 使用ASAP 2.0数据集，采用三种GPT-5变体（GPT-5、GPT-5 mini、GPT-5 nano）生成控制长度的摘要作为下游评分模型输入。同时从完整作文中提取手工设计的语言特征，与摘要表示结合形成混合框架。通过二次加权kappa评估评分可靠性，通过词汇重叠、语义相似度、信息保留和冗余度评估摘要质量。

Result: GPT-5 mini在人机评分一致性方面表现最佳，GPT-5产生最高质量的摘要。高质量作文的摘要质量下降，表明复杂写作更难压缩而不丢失信息。研究揭示了模型容量、摘要保真度、成本效率和教育结构保留之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 生成式AI摘要为可扩展的写作评估提供了有前景的途径，但需要仔细验证信息保留和公平性。本研究为GPT基摘要应用于自动作文评分提供了初步控制评估，确定了未来推广所需的重要基线和消融研究。

Abstract: Automated essay scoring (AES) enables scalable assessment and timely feedback but remains challenged by transformer input-length limitations, which can cause information loss when processing long essays. This study proposes a generative AI-assisted summarization framework to improve long-form essay representation while maintaining scoring reliability. Using the ASAP 2.0 dataset, we generate controlled-length summaries with three GPT-5 variants (GPT-5, GPT-5 mini, and GPT-5 nano) and use them as inputs for downstream AES models. To preserve original writing signals, handcrafted linguistic features extracted from full essays are integrated with summary representations to form a hybrid framework. The approach is evaluated in terms of scoring performance, summarization quality, and computational cost. Scoring reliability is measured using quadratic weighted kappa (QWK), while summary quality is assessed through lexical overlap, semantic similarity, information retention, and redundancy metrics. Results show that GPT-5 mini achieves the highest agreement with human ratings, whereas GPT-5 produces the strongest summarization quality. Summary quality decreases for higher-scoring essays, indicating that more complex writing is more difficult to compress without information loss. These findings reveal trade-offs among model capacity, summary fidelity, cost efficiency, and preservation of educational constructs. This study provides an initial controlled evaluation of GPT-based summarization for AES and identifies important baselines and ablation studies required for future generalization. Overall, generative AI summarization offers a promising approach for scalable writing assessment while requiring careful validation of information preservation and fairness.

</details>


### [88] [CAMMAR: Culture-Aware Matryoshka for Metaphorical Arabic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15847)
*Suzan Awinat,Alfonso Ortega del Puente*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAMMAR是一个用于阿拉伯语隐喻表示的文化感知表示学习框架，通过分阶段的语义课程将意义组织到嵌套的词汇、文化和隐喻嵌入子空间中，基于几何距离提供无训练的隐喻度量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前阿拉伯语语言模型通常将词汇、文化和隐喻信息压缩到单一的表示空间中，这种现象被称为"语义涂抹"，无法准确捕捉阿拉伯语中基于文化的隐喻机制。

Method: 引入CAMMAR框架，基于Al-Jurjani的nazum理论原理，通过分阶段语义课程学习嵌套的词汇、文化和隐喻嵌入子空间。设计基于词汇和隐喻表示之间距离的几何度量方法。

Result: 在带有成对监督的情况下，几何读取器能显著优于随机检测隐喻(AUC达0.84；在82.6%的词对中，隐喻得分高于其字面对应词)，但在无监督领域对比下效果仅为随机水平。词根基础层提供了稳定的小幅增益。

Conclusion: CAMMAR框架能够有效分离阿拉伯语中的词汇、文化和隐喻表示，提供了一种基于几何的无训练隐喻检测方法，在监督条件下表现良好，揭示了分层表示学习的重要性。

Abstract: Metaphor in Arabic is a culturally grounded mechanism for constructing meaning, encoding cultural knowledge that shapes interpretation. Yet current Arabic language models typically collapse lexical, cultural, and metaphorical information into a single representational space, a phenomenon we term "semantic smearing". We introduce CAMMAR (Culture-Aware Matryoshka for Metaphorical Arabic Representations), a representation learning framework that organizes meaning into nested lexical, cultural, and metaphorical embedding subspaces through a staged semantic curriculum. The design implements compositional principles of Al-Jurjani's theory of nazum, modeling figurative meaning as compositionally grounded in prior semantic relations, and yields a training-free geometric measure of metaphoricity based on the distance between lexical and metaphorical representations.
  Evaluated on a new span-annotated Arabic metaphor set as word-matched figurative/literal pairs, the geometric readout detects metaphor well above chance when the inter-layer geometry is shaped by paired supervision (AUC up to 0.84; figurative outscores its literal counterpart for the same word in 82.6\% of pairs), but sits at chance under an unsupervised domain contrast alone, a clean separation between a legible-under-supervision regime and a non-emergent one. A controlled ablation shows that grounding the lexical layer in morphological roots gives a small but consistent gain, an effect absent from direct probing that reflects the layer's quality as a measurement anchor. We will release the datasets, cultural concept inventory, and code upon acceptance.

</details>


### [89] [Contextual Semantic Relevance Tracks fMRI BOLD Responses During Naturalistic Speech Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15856)
*Kun Sun,Rong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现上下文语义相关性（而非惊奇值）能稳健预测自然语音理解过程中的fMRI BOLD反应，表明血流动力学反应对语义整合更敏感，扩展了语言理解的计算模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言理解需要听者处理局部的概率预期和上下文语义关系。虽然惊奇值（surprisal）被广泛用于量化局部词汇的意外性，但其在连续理解过程中能否稳健预测fMRI BOLD反应的证据并不一致。本研究旨在探讨上下文语义相关性（定义为传入词汇与其近期语义背景的关联强度）是否能在自然语音理解过程中预测BOLD反应。

Method: 研究分析了两个公开的fMRI数据集（Alice数据集和Moth数据集），将它们视为互补而非完全相同的重复。使用广义加性混合模型（GAMMs）对转换后的BOLD响应进行建模，并使用FIR/去卷积分析对原始连续BOLD时间序列进行测试。

Result: 在Alice数据集中，语义相关性在所有12个感兴趣区域（ROI）中均显著，而惊奇值在FDR校正后不显著。在Moth数据集中，语义相关性在所有30个ROI中均表现出一致的负效应，而惊奇值未显示可比模式。

Conclusion: 本研究得出结论，语义相关性是一个有前景的、对BOLD信号敏感的上下文语义适配度量指标。它扩展了语言理解的计算模型，从单纯的预测转向上下文敏感的语义整合，表明在自然语言理解中，缓慢的血流动力学反应对上下文语义整合特别敏感，而局部的概率预测误差可能更难通过fMRI可靠检测。

Abstract: Naturalistic language comprehension requires listeners to process both local probabilistic expectations and contextual semantic relations. Surprisal has been widely used to quantify local word unexpectedness, but evidence that it robustly predicts fMRI BOLD responses during continuous comprehension has been mixed. This study investigates whether contextual semantic relevance, defined as how strongly an incoming word relates to its recent semantic context, predicts BOLD responses during naturalistic speech comprehension. We analyzed two public fMRI datasets, the Alice dataset and the Moth dataset, treating them as complementary rather than identical replications. Transformed BOLD responses were modeled with generalized additive mixed models (GAMMs) and original continuous BOLD time series were tested with FIR/deconvolution analyses. In Alice, semantic relevance was significant across all 12 ROIs (region of interest), whereas surprisal was not significant after FDR correction. In Moth, semantic relevance showed consistent negative effects across all 30 ROIs, while surprisal showed no comparable pattern. These findings suggest that semantic relevance is a promising BOLD-sensitive metric of contextual semantic fit. More broadly, our findings support the view that slow hemodynamic responses during naturalistic speech comprehension may be especially sensitive to contextual semantic integration, whereas local probabilistic prediction error may be more difficult to detect reliably with fMRI. In this sense, semantic relevance extends computational models of language comprehension from prediction alone toward context-sensitive semantic integration.

</details>


### [90] [Conditional Reliability of Toxicity Signals for Multilingual and Code-Mixed Abuse Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15861)
*Indraveni Chebolu,Rohan Singh,Arnab Mallick,Harmesh Rana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToxGate通过条件化融合外部毒性先验，显著提升了多语言和语码混合短文本中的辱骂检测性能，特别是在高风险内容和跨数据集场景中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前内容审核系统日益依赖外部毒性检测工具，但这些工具在多语言混合、音译、俚语和语言不匹配的情况下不可靠。作者旨在研究毒性先验在印度多语言和语码混合短文本中的条件可靠性，并探索如何更有效地利用这些外部信号。

Method: 作者提出了ToxGate方法，这是一种信任融合头部（trust-fusion head），它将每个辅助信号（如英语毒性、印度语辱骂、基于规则的严重性线索）与编码器的表示进行条件化融合，然后将其添加到预测状态中。该方法在三个短文本辱骂数据集、四种Transformer编码器和每个设置五个种子的实验条件下进行了评估。

Result: 在12个域内设置中，ToxGate在10个设置中优于匹配的普通编码器；在8个迁移设置中，ToxGate在7个设置中表现更优。最大且最可解释的改进出现在高风险审核切片中，包括明确侮辱、暴力威胁和跨数据集迁移。在消融研究中，特定于源的选通机制在迁移、严重辱骂切片和高风险分类中表现最强。

Conclusion: 该论文得出结论，外部毒性检测工具在应对多语言和语码混合文本时存在局限性，应被视为条件性证据而非固定特征或绝对真实标签。ToxGate方法通过条件化融合机制有效提升了毒性检测的准确性和可解释性，特别是在高风险内容检测和跨数据集迁移场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Moderation systems increasingly rely on external toxicity tools, but those tools are unreliable under code-mixing, transliteration, slang, and language mismatch. We study the \emph{conditional reliability} of toxicity priors in Indian multilingual and code-mixed short text: English toxicity, Indic abuse, and rule-based severity cues can be useful evidence, but only in some linguistic and abuse-severity contexts. We propose ToxGate, a trust-fusion head that conditions each auxiliary signal on the encoder representation before adding it to the prediction state. Across three short-text abuse datasets, four transformer encoders, and five seeds per setting, ToxGate improves over matched plain encoders in 10 of 12 in-domain settings and 7 of 8 transfer settings. The largest and most interpretable gains occur in high-risk moderation slices, including explicit slurs, violent threats, and cross-dataset transfer. The broader lesson is that moderation systems should treat external toxicity tools and priors as conditional evidence rather than fixed features or ground truth, in focused ablations, source-specific gating gives the strongest results in transfer, severe-abuse slices, and high-risk triage.

</details>


### [91] [An MLIR-Based Compilation Method for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15865)
*Pengchao Hu,Zhibin Xin,Yifan Chen,Yangyang Zhou,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于MLIR的LLM编译方法，通过TopOp和TpuOp两个方言实现模型语义与硬件决策的分离，并拆分Transformer层为三个阶段以优化推理，已在TPU-MLIR和LLM-TPU项目中实现，支持多种模型和量化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型已成为现代AI加速器上的主要工作负载，但在专用硬件上部署仍面临两个核心挑战：如何将训练好的模型导入编译器友好的中间表示，以及如何在有限的片上内存下高效调度自回归推理循环。

Method: 提出基于MLIR的编译方法，使用两个方言：TopOp（高层图方言，独立于源框架和目标芯片，表达模型语义）和TpuOp（目标硬件方言，承载芯片相关决策，如量化、层组、内存布局）。模型首先表示为TopOp，然后逐层降至TpuOp，最终生成可部署二进制。此外，将每个Transformer层拆分为三个阶段进行静态编译：prefill、prefill_kv（带历史键值缓存的prefill）和decode，以适应提示并行处理和逐令牌生成的不同计算特性。

Result: 该方法已在TPU-MLIR编译器和LLM-TPU部署项目中实现，支持包括Qwen、Llama、InternVL和MiniCPM-V系列在内的多种生成模型，以及GPTQ、AWQ、AutoRound等多种量化和部署形式。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了在AI加速器上部署大型语言模型的核心挑战，通过MLIR中间表示和分层编译流程，实现了从模型到硬件的高效映射，为实际部署提供了灵活、高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become the dominant workload on modern AI accelerators, yet deploying them on specialized hardware still faces two core challenges: how to import a trained model into a compiler-friendly intermediate representation, and how to efficiently schedule the autoregressive inference loop under limited on-chip memory. This paper presents an MLIR (Multi-Level Intermediate Representation) based compilation method for large language models, illustrated using two dialects of operators, TopOp and TpuOp. TopOp serves as a high-level graph dialect that is independent of both the source framework and the target chip, and is responsible for expressing model semantics; TpuOp serves as the target hardware dialect, carrying chip-related decisions such as quantization, layer groups, and memory layout. A model is first represented as TopOp, then lowered layer by layer to TpuOp, and finally a deployable binary is generated. In addition, each Transformer layer is split into three stages for static compilation: prefill, prefill_kv (prefill with historical key-value cache), and decode, so as to accommodate the different computational characteristics of prompt-parallel processing and per-token generation. The method has been implemented in the TPU-MLIR compiler{https://github.com/sophgo/tpu-mlir} and the LLM-TPU deployment project\footnote{https://github.com/sophgo/LLM-TPU}, supporting a variety of generative models including the Qwen, Llama, InternVL, and MiniCPM-V series, as well as multiple quantization and deployment forms such as GPTQ, AWQ, and AutoRound.

</details>


### [92] [How Much Human Label Variation Does Formal Semantic Structure Explain?: Group-Level Effects and Item-Level Ceilings in NLI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15870)
*Haram Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明形式语义结构仅能解释小部分NLI任务中的标注者分歧，对高分歧项识别能力有限，且不影响分歧性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化形式语义结构对自然语言推理中人类标注者变异性的解释程度，将标注分歧视为信号而非噪声进行系统测量。

Method: 使用基于规则的算子与单调性标注器（MED验证），在ChaosNLI的3,113个SNLI和MNLI项目上进行分析，包含三个预注册分析模块，并完整报告阴性结果。

Result: 三个主要边界：1) 非纯向上单调假设显示更高标注熵；2) 形式特征仅解释3.3-3.6%的熵方差，AUC=0.606，无法识别高分歧项目；3) 三项高统计检验力的预注册对比均返回阴性结果，表明语义结构不改变分歧性质。

Conclusion: 在该样本中，形式语义结构仅轻微影响标注者分歧程度，未检测到对分歧内容的改变，所有结论限于低原始一致性的ChaosNLI-S/M项目范围。

Abstract: Human label variation in natural language inference is increasingly treated as signal rather than noise, but how much of it formal semantic structure explains has not been measured directly. We measure it on the 3,113 SNLI and MNLI items of ChaosNLI, using a rule-based operator and monotonicity tagger validated against MED (0.883 agreement at the edit site, 0.807 on the sentence-level summary our analyses consume), three preregistered analysis blocks, and full reporting of negative results. Three bounds emerge. First, a group-level boundary: hypotheses that are not purely upward monotone show reliably higher label entropy (Cliff's delta = -0.284), and rank-based tests defend the effect against operator-presence and length reductions, though a bounded-outcome sensitivity check weakens the regression form of the length defense. Second, an item-level ceiling: the same formal profiles explain only 3.3 to 3.6 percent of entropy variance and reach a median-split AUC of 0.606, too weak to identify high-disagreement items. Third, composition invariance: across the boundary, three high-powered preregistered contrasts on validated error shares and explanation-type shares (VariErr, LiTEx) all return null results. In this sample, formal semantic structure shifts how much annotators disagree by a small amount and does not detectably change what they disagree about. ChaosNLI-S/M consists of items selected for low original agreement, and every claim is conditioned on that scope. All analyses were preregistered in a version-controlled research log, whose audit trail, including one corrected interpretation rule, the paper discloses.

</details>


### [93] [DECODEM: Data Extraction from Corporate Organizational Documents via Enhanced Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15879)
*Jens Frankenreiter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DECODEM基准数据集，用于评估从公司文件中自动提取治理变量的方法，结果表明前沿模型能够高精度提取法律信息，自动特征提取在构建公司治理数据集中具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上依赖人工编码公司文件（如章程和章程细则）来提取结构化变量，成本高、难以扩展且不透明，需要自动化解决方案。

Method: 引入DECODEM基准数据集，评估多种基于大型语言模型的提取流程，包括不同提示设计、任务分解和文档处理策略。

Result: 自动化提取对于许多治理条款是可行的，中位性能接近方法上限；性能随条款系统性变化，少数条款导致大部分错误；更复杂的提示策略和级联流程对前沿模型无持续改进，但能缩小前沿模型与效率导向模型之间的差距。

Conclusion: 当前的前沿模型能够以高精度从复杂的公司文件中提取具有法律意义的信息，自动特征提取在构建公司治理数据集中具有重要未来作用。

Abstract: Much empirical legal research depends on translating unstructured text into structured variables. In corporate governance research as elsewhere, this translation has traditionally relied on human coding of documents such as charters and bylaws, a process that is costly, difficult to scale, and often opaque. This paper introduces DECODEM, a set of benchmark datasets for evaluating the automated extraction of corporate governance variables from organizational documents. The benchmarks pair randomly sampled corporate charters and bylaws with high-quality human annotations covering a range of governance provisions commonly studied in empirical work.
  Using these datasets, the paper evaluates several large-language-model extraction pipelines that vary in prompt design, task decomposition, and document handling. The underlying task consists of a set of document-level binary classification problems, one for each governance variable. The results show that automated extraction is feasible at a high level of accuracy for many provisions, with median performance near the upper bound across approaches. At the same time, performance varies systematically across variables, with a small number of provisions accounting for most of the remaining errors. More elaborate prompting strategies and cascading pipelines do not consistently improve performance for frontier models, but substantially narrow the gap between frontier and efficiency-oriented models in some settings, suggesting that pipeline design can partly substitute for model capability.
  By providing a standardized benchmark and a systematic evaluation of extraction methods, the paper demonstrates that current frontier models can extract legally meaningful information from complex corporate documents with high accuracy and suggests an important future role for automated feature extraction in constructing corporate governance datasets.

</details>


### [94] [Induction in Both Directions: A Mechanistic Analysis of In-Context Learning in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15893)
*Andy Catruna,Emilian Radoi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 扩散语言模型学习双向归纳电路，与自回归模型相比在双向上下文可见时表现出更强的归纳能力，并能隐式计算掩码标记的全局比例作为时间步信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自回归变换器的内部机制已被广泛研究，但新兴的扩散语言模型（通过迭代去噪生成文本）的内部工作机制尚不清楚。本研究旨在探究DLMs如何实现归纳机制——这是一种上下文学习中的关键机制，模型通过寻找重复的上下文并复制其后继标记来完成学习。

Method: 分析比较了注意力仅自回归模型和吸收掩码扩散语言模型，采用匹配的架构设计。研究重点关注DLMs如何通过双向归纳电路实现归纳机制，包括前向和后向标记头如何将局部上下文写入残差流，以及随后的归纳头如何使用这些信息从匹配的源位置寻找和复制答案。

Result: DLMs学习了一种双向对称的归纳电路，无论源位置出现在过去还是未来都能工作。当只有左侧上下文可见时（类似于AR模型），DLM在归纳能力上并未超越其AR对应模型。然而，当掩码标记两侧的上下文都可见时，DLM表现出更强的归纳能力，这归因于双向上下文访问而非单侧机制增强。此外，DLMs能够隐式计算掩码标记的全局比例并将其作为隐式时间步，即使没有提供显式的时间步嵌入。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型通过独特的双向归纳电路实现归纳机制，在双向上下文访问条件下比自回归模型具有优势。更重要的是，DLMs能够隐式地从输入数据中推导出时间步信息，这为其内部工作机制提供了新的理解，并揭示了与自回归模型在架构和功能上的重要差异。

Abstract: While the internal mechanisms of autoregressive (AR) transformers have been studied extensively, much less is known about diffusion language models (DLMs), an emerging alternative that generates text by iterative denoising. In this work, we study how DLMs implement induction, a mechanism behind in-context learning in which the model finds a repeated context and copies the token that followed it. Our analysis compares attention-only AR models and absorbing-mask DLMs with matched architectures. We find that DLMs learn a bidirectional induction circuit, where previous-token and next-token heads write local context into the residual stream and later induction heads use it to find and copy the answer from the matching source position. The circuit is direction-symmetric, working whether the source appears in the past or in the future. When only left context is visible, matching what an AR model sees, the DLM does not outperform its AR counterpart in induction capabilities. However, we observe it has stronger induction when both sides of the masked token are visible, pointing to bidirectional context access rather than a stronger one-sided mechanism. Beyond induction, we provide causal evidence that DLMs compute the global fraction of masked tokens and use it as an implicit timestep, even though they are given no explicit timestep embedding.

</details>


### [95] [From Plausible to Actionable: A Position on LLM Self-Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15957)
*Elize Herrewijnen,Benedetta Muscato,Gizem Gezici,Fosca Giannotti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该观点论文探讨了LLM生成自我解释的可靠性问题，提出虽然这些解释往往看似合理，但其忠实性存疑，主张评价应扩展到可操作性，并为评估提供了实用指南


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的自我解释已成为可解释AI的重要方向，但这类解释看似合理却不一定忠实地反映模型的真实推理过程，需要对现有评估方法进行反思

Method: 从传统XAI角度分析标准评估协议的限制，提出评估LLM自我解释的实用指南，强调评价应超越合理性和忠实性，扩展到可操作性

Result: 识别了LLM自我解释的三个关键特性：高度合理、忠实性存疑、高度可操作，为评估提供了框架和指导原则

Conclusion: LLM自我解释具有独特价值，但其评估需要更全面的框架，不仅要关注合理性和忠实性，更要重视可操作性，以实现对多样化利益相关者决策的支持

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate natural language explanations that rationalize their own decisions, a phenomenon commonly referred to as self-explanations.Such explanations have emerged as a promising direction for explainable artificial intelligence (XAI), particularly for interpreting LLM behavior.However, while self-explanations often appear plausible, whether they faithfully reflect a model's underlying reasoning process remains an open question. In this opinion paper, we argue that self-explanations can be highly plausible, questionably faithful, and yet highly actionable. From a traditional XAI perspective, we identify the limitations of standard evaluation protocols for LLM-generated self-explanations and propose practical guidelines for assessing their plausibility and faithfulness. Moreover, we argue that evaluation should extend beyond these criteria to actionability, highlighting applications of LLM rationalization capabilities that support informed decision-making and appropriate action across diverse stakeholders.

</details>


### [96] [BayesPO: Bayesian Prompt Optimization via Parallel-Tempered Gradient-Guided Discrete MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16001)
*Junjie Zhou,Zhijian Ou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BayesPO：一种基于贝叶斯后验采样的提示优化框架，将提示优化转化为离散标记上的能量采样问题，使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法搜索最优提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动提示优化器大多是启发式搜索过程，缺乏理论基础。本文旨在为提示优化提供更严谨的概率框架，通过将提示优化建模为离散提示标记上的贝叶斯后验采样问题，结合任务似然和语言模型先验，实现更系统的优化方法。

Method: 提出了BayesPO框架，将提示优化定义为贝叶斯后验采样问题，使用Gibbs-with-Langevin提议和Metropolis-Hastings校正的MCMC方法，集成并行回火技术以探索LLM诱导的能量景观，并针对非权重绑定LLM嵌入的实践约束进行了适配。

Result: 在诊断任务中发现语义有意义的提示；在诗歌完成任务中并行回火有助于逃离局部最优；在24个指令归纳子任务上，对APE提示进行后优化将平均准确率从60.04%提升至63.23%。但也发现两个主要局限：能量最小化可能在小优化集上过拟合，当前采样器计算成本较高。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯提示采样作为一种原则性后优化工具具有前景，为概率提示优化指明了一个有前途的方向，尽管当前实现存在计算成本高的限制。

Abstract: Prompt optimization adapts large language models (LLMs) without updating model parameters, but many automatic prompt optimizers remain heuristic search procedures over candidate instructions. This paper studies prompt optimization as Bayesian posterior sampling over discrete prompt tokens. We define a posterior distribution by combining a task likelihood term, which rewards prompts that explain input-output examples, with a language-model prior, which favors fluent instructions. This converts prompt optimization into an energy-based posterior sampling problem, for which gradients can be used to guide discrete Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) proposals over vocabulary tokens. We refer to our framework as BayesPO, short for Bayesian Prompt Optimization. In this paper, BayesPO is instantiated with Markov chain Monte Carlo: it uses a Metropolis-Hastings corrected Gibbs-with-Langevin (GwL) proposal and integrates parallel tempering for global exploration of rugged LLM-induced energy landscapes. The concrete sampler further adapts the GwL sampler to the practical constraints of non-weight-tied LLM embeddings. Experiments with Qwen2.5 models show that the sampler discovers semantically meaningful prompts on diagnostic tasks, that parallel tempering helps escape a local optimum in a poetry completion task, and that post-optimizing APE prompts on 24 instruction-induction subtasks improves average accuracy from 60.04% to 63.23%. The study also reveals two main limitations: energy minimization may overfit small optimization sets, and the current sampler remains computationally expensive. These findings position Bayesian prompt sampling as a principled post-optimization tool and point to a promising direction for probabilistic prompt optimization.

</details>


### [97] [Candidate Attended Dialogue State Tracking Using BERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16021)
*Junyuan Zheng,Onkar Salvi,John Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于预训练BERT模型的可扩展多领域对话状态跟踪框架，能够实现零样本泛化，无需额外训练即可快速适应新领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向对话系统中，对话状态跟踪（DST）是核心组件。随着Google Assistant、Siri、Alexa等对话系统应用需要支持大量服务和API，系统可扩展性受到关注。特别是对于训练数据稀少或没有训练数据的领域，需要能够迁移其他领域现有知识的能力。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的可扩展多领域对话状态跟踪框架。该系统利用预训练的BERT模型实现零样本泛化，能够快速适应新领域而无需额外训练。

Result: 在最近发布的基于模式对话（SGD）数据集上评估了模型性能，结果显示相比先前基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了基于预训练模型的可扩展对话状态跟踪框架的有效性，能够为零样本领域适应提供解决方案，解决了对话系统在实际应用中需要支持大量领域时的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Dialogue state tracking (DST) is one of the core components in task-oriented dialogue systems. At each turn in a conversation, DST estimates the user belief or dialogue state, which is used as input for downstream modules to predict system actions and generate responses. The increasingly popular dialogue system applications like Google Assistant, Siri and Alexa need to support a large number of services and APIs, resulting in growing attention to the scalability of such systems. Especially for some domains with little or no training data, the capability of transferring existing knowledge of other domains is highly desired. In this paper, we present a novel scalable framework for multi-domain dialogue state tracking. The proposed system leverages the pretrained BERT model to achieve zero-shot generalization, making it easy to quickly adapt to new domains without additional training. The performance of our model is evaluated on recently released schema-based dialogue (SGD) dataset, showing significant improvement compared to previous baseline.

</details>


### [98] [Loop the Loopies!](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16051)
*Zitian Gao,Yilong Chen,Yihao Xiao,Xinyu Yang,Ran Tao,Joey Zhou,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Loopie是当前最强大的循环Transformer模型，包含20B和6B两个MoE版本，通过新颖的循环架构和训练方法，在同等计算预算下显著优于标准Transformer基线，并在IMO和IPhO竞赛中获得金牌级表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer长期以来面临一个核心挑战：给定N倍预训练计算量的增加，将模型参数量增加N倍通常优于将模型循环N次。Loopie旨在解决这一挑战，探索在有限计算资源下如何通过循环架构实现更优性能。

Method: Loopie采用混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts）架构，包含20B参数（2B活跃参数）和6B参数（0.6B活跃参数）两个版本。通过创新的循环Transformer设计，配合新型的后训练流程，增强了模型的推理能力。

Result: 广泛的消融研究表明，Loopie在同等计算预算下显著优于标准Transformer基线（包括30B-A3B模型）。在2025年IMO（国际数学奥林匹克）和IPhO（国际物理奥林匹克）竞赛中，Loopie无需工具即达到金牌级性能。

Conclusion: Loopie成功解决了循环Transformer长期存在的性能瓶颈问题，证明了通过创新的架构设计和训练方法，循环Transformer可以在有限计算资源下超越标准Transformer，并为解决复杂推理问题提供了有效路径。

Abstract: We present Loopie, the most powerful looped Transformer to date. The Loopie series consists of two Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models: a 20B-parameter model with 2B active parameters and a 6Bparameter model with 0.6B active parameters. Looped Transformers have long faced a challenge: given an N-fold increase in pre-training compute, increasing the parameter count by a factor of N usually outperforms looping a model N times. Loopie addresses this challenge. Extensive ablation studies, including comparisons with a vanilla 30B-A3B model, show that Loopie substantially outperforms vanilla Transformer baselines trained with the same compute budget. Our novel post-training pipeline equips Loopie with strong reasoning abilities. At the 2025 IMO and IPhO, Loopie achieves gold-medal performance without tools.

</details>


### [99] [Frontier AI performance across the business disciplines: a case-grounded benchmark of knowledge work and analytical reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16057)
*Ajay Patel,Kartik Hosanagar,Ramayya Krishnan,Chris Callison-Burch,Karim Lakhani,Mitch Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究者创建了BusinessCaseBench基准来评估大语言模型在商业案例分析等复杂知识工作上的能力，发现前沿AI模型已表现出色且能力快速提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI基准主要测试事实回忆、数学解题、编码等能力，但缺乏对商业专业人士日常分析性知识工作的评估，特别是复杂信息综合、不确定性下的判断、战略思维、权衡分析等主观性较强的能力。

Method: 借鉴顶级商学院使用的"案例教学法"，构建BusinessCaseBench基准，包含来自18个商业学科的数百个问题，每个问题配有源自专家编写的教学案例解决方案的评分标准。

Result: 在BusinessCaseBench上，前沿AI模型已经能够根据教师评分标准获得高分，且同一模型系列在两年内能力显著提升，表明AI在这类工作上的性能已很高且快速改进。

Conclusion: AI在复杂分析性知识工作上的能力已达到高水平并持续提升，这对商学院的案例教学法和入门级专业职位有重要启示，因为这些领域历来依赖此类技能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are improving rapidly as reflected in benchmark scores, yet these AI benchmarks largely test capabilities such as factual recall, narrow question answering, mathematical problem-solving, and coding and agentic tool-use. What remains poorly measured is AI progress on the analytical knowledge work white-collar professionals perform daily, including synthesizing complex information, exercising judgment under uncertainty and incomplete information, applying strategic and adversarial thinking in multi-stakeholder settings, weighing trade-offs, and producing defensible, structured analyses. This gap is even more pronounced for subjective components of such work, where success can be challenging to define. The "case method" form of education practiced by top business schools provides a natural foundation for addressing this measurement gap, and we construct BusinessCaseBench, a benchmark spanning hundreds of questions drawn from business cases across eighteen disciplines, each paired with a grading rubric derived from the expert-written instructor case solution. On BusinessCaseBench, frontier AI models already score highly against instructor rubrics, and capability within one model family improves substantially over two years. These results provide strong evidence that AI performance on this class of work is already high and rapidly improving, with implications for business schools, where case pedagogy trains undergraduates and MBAs in this kind of analytical reasoning, and for entry-level professional roles, where such skills have historically anchored early-career work.

</details>


### [100] [Frontier Language Models Struggle to Copy: Text Can Be Better Viewed in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16072)
*Haodong Wen,Yiran Zhang,Yingfa Chen,Kaifeng Lyu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现即使先进的大语言模型也无法准确复制输入字符串，归因于Transformer位置编码的局限性。为此，作者提出了2D-RoPE位置编码，将文本视为二维网格，使复制任务简化为按列偏移检索，显著提升了复制性能，并在合成和大规模预训练实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLMs）能够解决高级推理问题，但即使是前沿模型也无法准确执行一个更简单的操作：精确复制位于其上下文窗口内的输入字符串。作者将这种失败归因于Transformer架构中的位置编码，其归纳偏置倾向于通过匹配局部上下文的捷径进行复制，而非精确定位对应的输入位置。

Method: 提出2D-RoPE位置编码方法，将文本视为二维网格而非一维序列，为每个标记分配行ID和列ID。在这种视角下，复制任务简化为按固定列偏移检索输入标记，使模型易于学习。在合成复制实验中，使用2D-RoPE的浅层Transformer能够在训练长度数百倍的输入上实现完美复制，而标准位置编码则表现不佳。

Result: 在合成复制实验中，使用2D-RoPE的浅层Transformer能够在输入长度达到训练长度数百倍的情况下实现完美复制，而标准位置编码则远远落后。此外，在大规模预训练（DCLM数据集，模型规模达1.4B参数）中，2D-RoPE语言模型在复制任务上的优势持续存在。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入2D-RoPE位置编码，将文本组织为二维网格结构，成功解决了Transformer模型在简单复制任务上的失败问题。实验证明，该方法在合成复制任务和大规模预训练中均表现出色，能够实现远超训练长度的完美复制，且可扩展至1.4B参数模型。这表明二维文本视角对语言建模具有显著益处，为未来探索二维位置编码的潜力提供了新方向。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) can solve advanced reasoning problems in seconds, we show that even frontier models fail to perform a much simpler operation: exactly copying an input string that lies well within their context windows. We attribute this failure to positional encodings in Transformer architectures, whose inductive bias favors copying through a shortcut based on matching local contexts rather than carefully locating the corresponding input positions. To address this issue, we introduce 2D-RoPE, which organizes text into a 2D grid rather than a 1D sequence and assigns each token a row ID and a column ID. Under this view, copying becomes simply retrieving input tokens at a fixed column offset, which makes the task easy to learn. In synthetic copy experiments, shallow Transformers with 2D-RoPE achieve perfect copying at input lengths hundreds of times longer than those seen during training, whereas standard positional encodings fall far behind. We further show that the advantage of 2D-RoPE language models on copy tasks consistently holds in large-scale pretraining on DCLM with model sizes up to 1.4B parameters. Overall, our results suggest that viewing text in 2D can benefit language modeling, and we hope this encourages future work to further explore the potential of 2D positional encodings.

</details>


### [101] [Controlling Implicit Shortcut Reliance in L2 Spoken English Auto-markers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16085)
*Shilin Gao,Mark J. F. Gales,Kate M. Knill*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种新的训练准则来减少语言评估系统中分类器对"捷径"特征的过度依赖，防止学习者通过利用这些捷径而非真正提高语言能力来提高分数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语音和语言处理系统使用复杂的深度学习方法直接从原始音频或文本输入进行任务处理，但这些系统容易学习"捷径"——分类器过度依赖输入的特定方面来产生输出。在语言熟练度评估任务中，这种过度依赖使得学习者可以通过利用捷径而非真正提高语言能力来提高分数，导致评估不公

Method: 引入一种新颖的训练准则来减少分类器对捷径的依赖。该方法在两种评估系统中进行了验证：一种基于音频输入，另一种基于语音识别文本。通过修改训练标准来限制对可被滥用的特征的过度依赖

Result: 实验结果显示，两种系统（音频基和文本基）与可能被滥用的特征之间存在高于人类参考的相关性，表明系统确实过度依赖这些特征。通过引入修改后的训练准则，这种相关性可以降低到更接近参考相关性的水平

Conclusion: 提出的训练准则能有效减少语言评估系统中分类器对捷径特征的过度依赖，有助于提高评估的公平性和有效性，防止学习者通过不当手段提高分数，确保评估结果反映真实的语言能力

Abstract: Increasingly, speech and language processing tasks take either audio or text directly rather than extracting features from these as the input to the classifier or regressor. Often these systems make use of complex, for example transformer-based, processes that have the ability to derive highly non-linear mappings between the input and the output. Unfortunately these systems can also learn ''shortcuts'' where the classifier is overly reliant on particular aspects of the input to yield the output. For the task of language proficiency assessment, this over-reliance can enable learners to increase their score by exploiting the shortcut rather than improving their ability. This paper introduces a novel training criterion that is able to reduce the classifier's reliance on shortcuts, thus for example limiting this option for malpractice in language assessment. This process is illustrated on two forms of assessment system, one based on the audio the other on the speech recognition text. The results show that, for both systems, there is higher correlations with features that could be exploited for malpractice than expected from the human reference, indicating an over-reliance on these features. By introducing the modified training criterion, this correlation can be reduced to be closer to the reference correlation.

</details>


### [102] [Rate-Utility Frontiers for Language Encodings: Comparing Tokens, Bytes, and Pixels Under Controlled Linguistic Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16117)
*Ingo Ziegler,Martin Krebs,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过比较子词标记、原始字节和渲染像素三种编码方式在可控容量下的表现，研究发现编码选择不是固定的偏好，而是取决于任务、语言组合、容量限制和计算预算的速率-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通常使用不同的编码方式（子词标记、原始字节、渲染像素）处理文本，但这些编码在不同语言中暴露给模型的语义信息量不同。本研究旨在控制内容和下游容量的前提下，探讨每种编码方式实际保留了哪些信息。

Method: 使用13种语言5种文字体系的已验证平行句库，通过共享瓶颈结构比较标记、字节和像素编码。通过扫描瓶颈宽度来追踪速率-效用前沿，分离输入位置数量、编码后的潜在容量以及压缩后保留的任务相关信息三个常被混淆的维度。评估三种效用：表层形式保留、跨语言句子对齐和主题分类。

Result: 没有任何编码在所有任务或容量范围内占优：像素在保留表层形式方面最佳；字节在跨语言对齐方面最佳，尤其是在相同文字的多语言场景中；标记在主题预测方面最佳。这些性能不能仅用序列长度解释。短输入可能丢弃有用语义，而长输入可能保留易于压缩的信息。

Conclusion: 选择编码方式不是对标记、字节或像素的固定偏好，而是取决于具体任务、语言组合、容量限制和计算预算的速率-效用权衡。编码决策需要考虑多个交互因素而非单一指标。

Abstract: Language models encode text as subword tokens, raw bytes, or rendered pixels, but these encodings are usually compared under modeling constraints that expose different amounts of linguistic content to models across different languages. We instead ask what each encoding preserves when both the content and the downstream capacity are controlled. Using verified parallel sentences across thirteen languages and five scripts, we compare tokens, bytes, and pixels through a shared bottleneck whose width is swept to trace rate-utility frontiers. This separates three quantities that are often conflated: the number of input positions an encoding creates, the latent capacity available after encoding, and the task-relevant information that survives compression. We evaluate three utilities: surface form preservation, cross-lingual sentence alignment, and topic classification. No encoding dominates across tasks or capacity regimes. Pixels preserve surface form best, bytes preserve cross-lingual alignment best, especially in same-script multilingual settings, and tokens support topic prediction best. These performances are not explained by sequence length alone. Short inputs can discard useful meaning, while long inputs can preserve information that compresses well. Choosing an encoding is therefore not a fixed preference for tokens, bytes, or pixels, but a rate-utility tradeoff that depends on the task, language mix, capacity regime, and compute budget.

</details>


### [103] [ToolSciVer: Multimodal Scientific Claim Verification with Visual Tool Augmented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16131)
*Binglin Zhou,Peng Shi,Ryo Kamoi,Nan Zhang,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToolSciVer是一个工具增强的多模态科学声明验证框架，通过类型感知的视觉工具和GRPO训练策略，显著提升了从科学论文中验证声明的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态科学声明验证方法存在三个主要问题：1) 难以定位决定性的视觉证据；2) 无法准确读取结构化的科学可视化内容；3) 不能有效整合多模态观察结果进行可靠推理。这些问题限制了模型在实际科学声明验证中的准确性和实用性。

Method: 1) 提出了ToolSciVer框架，为视觉语言模型配备三种类型感知的视觉工具：表格行列聚焦、图表到结构解析、高分辨率区域放大，将密集的科学可视化内容转换为明确的、面向声明的证据。2) 采用组相对策略优化进行训练，使用复合奖励函数，包括答案正确性、格式有效性、长度控制、工具使用效率和工具有效性惩罚。

Result: 在SciVer和MuSciClaims数据集上，使用来自三个模型家族（Qwen、InternVL、Gemma）的五个视觉语言模型进行实验。结果表明，ToolSciVer方法相比四种竞争基线（包括基于提示的和基于强化学习的工具使用方法）取得了优越的性能，突显了学习的、类型感知的工具使用在科学声明验证中的有效性。

Conclusion: ToolSciVer通过类型感知的视觉工具和优化的训练策略，有效解决了多模态科学声明验证中的关键挑战，为科学声明验证提供了更可靠和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Scientific Claim Verification (MSCV) requires models to verify scientific claims using visually grounded evidence from papers, including figures, tables, charts, and textual context. However, existing methods often fail because they struggle to locate decisive visual evidence, accurately read structured scientific visuals, and integrate multimodal observations into reliable reasoning. We introduce ToolSciVer, the first tool-augmented framework for MSCV to our knowledge. ToolSciVer equips a VLM with three type-aware visual tools, table row/column focus, chart-to-structure parsing, and high-resolution region zoom, which convert dense scientific visuals into explicit, claim-facing evidence, and trains the policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) under a composite reward of answer correctness, format validity, length control, tool-use efficiency, and tool-validity penalties. Experiments on SciVer and MuSciClaims datasets on five VLMs from three model families (Qwen, InternVL, Gemma) demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance compared to four competitive baselines including prompting-based and RL-based tool-use methods, highlighting the effectiveness of learned, type-aware tool use for scientific claim verification.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [GraphDx: A Cost-Aware Knowledge-Enhanced Multi-Agent Framework for Sequential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15280)
*Shaoting Tan,Ning Liu,Yuntao Du,Shuyue Wei,Wu Shuai,Qian Li,Yanyu Xu,Wei Zhang,Lizhen Cui,Haitao Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphDx是一个知识增强框架，通过构建医学诊断知识图谱和协同代理系统，显著提升LLM在序列诊断中的准确性和成本效益


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法存在关键的知识推理差距：尽管编码了广泛的医学知识，但难以在成本约束下进行系统性推理，常常导致过度测试。需要解决诊断准确性与资源成本之间的平衡问题。
